# Operator Fallow verifier continuation admission

## Authority and interpretation

On 2026-09-15 the user replied verbatim after the concrete, reviewed same-round 1,500-launch proposal:

> [@Computer](plugin://computer-use@openai-bundled) you can proceed and finish the remaining work please

Root interprets this response in context as approval of that finite continuation and requested Computer Use work. The reply does not itself restate the numbers; this document preserves the actual words and identifies the preceding proposal as the scope. Grant ID: `operator-fallow-round-1-continuation-2026-09-15`.

## Boundaries

Continue round 1 on `codex/operator-fallow-refactor` from the original 30 spent supervised launches, capped at **1,500 cumulative launches**, **120 seconds per launch**, and **two semantic mutants per canonical case including original attempts**. Baselines, retries and verifier-suffix runs all spend the same ceiling. No additional round, product change, existing-assertion change, global limit change or accepted-risk disposition is granted.

The original ordered 501-case identities and four E2E exclusion reasons form an immutable prefix. Only the exact finite 15-case verifier-test suffix in the manifest may append; aliases cannot reset candidate counts. The separately approved first 287 and additional 68 discovery corrections below may change only their specified never-measured rows from historical `skipped-budget` to `excluded`, with the exact pinned reasons and call-path proofs. Preserve original journal entries and the historical report. The original JSON remains immutable at the report checkpoint in Git; final JSON contains one row per identity in the same order, with only these 355 specifically approved discovery corrections and outcome upgrades corroborated by valid continuation evidence, while attempt history remains append-only in Markdown and the journal. The original Markdown must be an exact prefix of the final Markdown. Original measured pins remain historical; final pins identify the admitted continuation.

Each new launch must bind admission, canonical candidate, phase, semantic attempt, escaped exact-one selector, target-original/mutant hashes, supervisor and digested results. A killed upgrade needs a matching green baseline and assertion-failing mutant; setup/import errors are inconclusive. This verifier does not admit equivalent upgrades; cases without a corroborated kill remain incomplete. Descriptive text alone cannot upgrade an outcome. Missing, changed or ambiguous old launch mappings fail admission.

Grant lineage must reject reset journals, stripped authorization, branch renames, lower round labels, `round-start`, or generic-budget fallback. The global ordinary-branch limit remains 30. Scrubbed environment, localhost-only DB, dotenv journal, watcher refusal, shell-free arguments, supervisor cleanup and root finalization remain binding. Root finalizes before report writes; foreign bytes abort restoration. Unresolved oracle/infra or skipped cases remain incomplete.

## Historical evidence and scheduling

The product baseline is `2ac6ff871b4496f996c37aaaaade9bdc7eee9a62`; the original report checkpoint is `8660fd1d01e35c22b9142b4fc2a020c05946f1c5`. The old journal records 30 launches and 15 mutations. The report records 10 killed, 5 inconclusive, 482 skipped-budget and 4 excluded, with RT-001 rejected as wrong-target and retained as an inconclusive attempt. No product defect was established.

Scheduling commit `15a5f447f9c415be5671ddd432917f472f939b9f` changes only the extra coverage command's workers from 2 to 1. Its successor `8ea2ac28f49724dd6df65435fe956d781dd72638` selects the existing integration project and fresh `.feature/operator-integration-coverage/full` output, merging that directory into ordinary unit coverage. Tests, timeouts, assertions, wait/check logic and product files remain unchanged. Admission checks these exact deltas; historical intake hashes are not rewritten. The ordinary 32-check gate is green at the latter commit; red-team completion is a separate condition.

The four published axis-report blobs are immutable admission evidence. The current status checker considers their publication outside the old report-only exception stale; this admission may validate those exact blobs, never a generic documentation exemption.

## Safeguard validation

Independent source review passed for the preceding prepared guard revision. Its two runner/status suites passed **318/318 tests in 204.67 seconds**, including the finite safeguards. After updating the exact 287 approved discovery corrections, the focused continuation selection passed **15/15 tests in 9.32 seconds**, including admitted baseline launch 31, mutant launch 32, restoration and corroborated report evidence in an isolated fixture; typecheck, lint, syntax and diff checks passed. Root and independent review of this final admission delta remain required. These checks cover exact-cap enforcement, prefix/suffix identity preservation, candidate-bound evidence, timeout/attempt limits, alias/reset/lineage refusal, ordinary-branch budgets and restoration. Existing assertions remain intact. The exact 15 new identities `rt-0502`–`rt-0516` are pinned below. This narrow validation is not a full-gate result or a completed mutation campaign. Root alone owns commit, state synchronization, gate and mutation scheduling.

## Specific discovery-correction approval; admission remains inactive

After reviewing the concrete question referencing `/private/tmp/operator-discovery-correction-proposal.md`, the user explicitly replied:

> Approve the exact 287 documented discovery corrections

This specific approval supersedes the earlier automatic-review refusal to publish classifications under only the broad finish-work instruction. It covers exactly the 287 ordered `discoveryCorrections` rows below and their pinned call-path proofs. The proposal JSON SHA-256 is `f8b5181724be926ee0d6a732d56b40a2c62678d824e76ca161176e12e81de637`; the canonical correction-row SHA-256 is `cb2c6819986e93c46e4a9c928c83974429696e00044404b78ac50a67e22c6c60`. The manifest records the exact approval separately from the launch-budget approval.

All 287 rows were `skipped-budget` at the historical checkpoint; none was previously measured. Correcting those discovery false positives retains 210 original candidates in scope, the unchanged four original E2E exclusions, and all 501 original identities and historical outcomes in the immutable checkpoint. The 15 admitted verifier safeguards append to that inventory and share the same launch ceiling. This is a finite discovery correction, not a coverage waiver, broader exclusion rule, new budget, product change, or permission to alter existing assertions.

The real findings reports and journal remain untouched: **round 1, 30 launches, fully restored**. This document prepares the approved correction manifest; immutable admission is still inactive until root completes review, independent review and the admission commit. No continuation launch or completed mutation campaign is claimed.

## Historical reconstruction repair before the first continuation launch

The unpublished candidate `11462125132d2d3c1cf968f764f64b927441b208` passed its full gate but failed admission validation before any continuation launch. The validator incorrectly required every historical replacement token to occur once. The retained `rt-0127` mutation replaced the first of two occurrences; `rt-0142` replaced the second. Both exact transformations reproduce their pinned historical mutant blobs and SHA-256 values. Independent review confirmed that the remaining 13 transformations and all later admission predicates pass.

The correction permits only those two candidate IDs, their exact recorded source/replacement text, exactly two occurrences and the recorded first/second selection. Full mutant equality and all existing blob, digest, journal and report pins remain required. Eleven assertions appended inside an existing verifier case cover both valid transformations and invalid positions, both-occurrence replacement, wrong identity/token, excess occurrences and the ordinary unique-token path. No original assertion, inventory identity, measured result or launch counter changes.

Root preserves the unpublished candidate under `refs/heads/codex/operator-admission-pre-repair-11462125` before amending its own admission commit. This happens with the original 30-launch journal closed and no continuation grant entry. The corrected committed admission must pass its gate and actual admission validation before the first new launch; no generic successor or post-launch admission exception is added.

## Finite UI assertion formats before the first continuation launch

Source review found that the ordinary assertion predicate did not recognize genuine jest-dom matcher failures or direct Testing Library observations. Root ran four deliberately failing synthetic framework tests outside the repository, with a sterile environment and no product imports, database access or campaign state. The installed Vitest 4.1.7 reporter serialized extended matchers and asynchronous queries as `Error`, and synchronous missing-element queries as `TestingLibraryElementError`. These format probes receive no mutation credit and do not change the historical journal.

The correction recognizes only thirteen independently reviewed frontend assertion sites. Each binds the existing candidate ID/full title/test file and source SHA-256, exact source line, target original blob, exact planned mutant SHA-256, diagnostic and framework dependency frames. The final stack must identify the direct pinned test frame; unknown layouts, setup/helper frames, wrong hashes, wrong diagnostics and mixed non-assertion failures remain rejected. An exception class alone is insufficient. Existing baseline, result/supervisor digest, identity, environment, attempt, timeout and restoration checks remain required, followed by root semantic review of actual results.

Safeguard assertions append inside the existing finite verifier cases, without changing any original assertion or suffix identity. The preceding unpublished `3dbc804f32178c6d69043d0bc94d52661b343116` candidate passed its full gate and actual admission check and is retained under `codex/operator-admission-pre-ui-classifier-3dbc804f` before amendment. The journal still contains exactly the original 30 closed launches with no continuation grant entry. The corrected final admission needs focused safeguard checks, independent review, actual admission validation and a fresh full gate before measurement. This adds no exclusions, mutation budget, successor admission, product behavior or risk waiver.

## D6 — Failed-file accounting repair after the six-case pilot

The used admission `03502fdcee60e61b891636a625c95c6a9dadec9b` remains immutable. Its six pilot pairs consumed launches 31–42; all original measurements and inconclusive suggestions remain unchanged. Vitest 4.1.7 counts nested describe suites in `numFailedTestSuites`; all six mutants failed one file and one assertion but reported two failed suites. The verifier now counts failed files from `testResults` and refuses nonempty file-level error messages. The existing artifact, supervisor, exact-test, semantic assertion, environment and source pins remain required.

This repair is within the already approved verifier support scope and the user's persistent instruction to finish. It is not a new owner approval, budget grant, discovery exclusion, product change or round. The full 42-launch checkpoint and original grant are preserved. This one direct-child control revision binds launches 43+ to corrected source blobs; original receipts remain bound to their original controller. Reports record separate corrective reevaluations against the same retained artifacts, without rewriting suggestions or claiming new executions. The cumulative 1500-launch and two-attempt ceilings remain unchanged.

### D7 — finite verifier correction within the existing approved support scope

The used admission `03502fdcee60e61b891636a625c95c6a9dadec9b` and used controller `e3bd968ae9fca8e0d787291cf067c266e67d89f9` remain immutable. This sole direct corrective child preserves the complete restored checkpoint above, the original grant, all launch and mutation prefixes, round 1, the cumulative 1,500-launch ceiling, the two-mutant limit, all 501 original identities, the 15 verifier suffix identities, and the 355 approved discovery corrections. The existing 42-launch failed-file correction and its six separate reevaluations remain bound to their original controller.

Only `rt-0103` receives an anchored, escaped full-name selector for new launches. Its original unanchored baseline executed two tests and remains inconclusive, retained and counted.
